  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "yauhqueme-c01",
      "source_id": "yauhqueme-s1",
      "quote": "Yauhqueme (del náhuatl: yauhqueme ‘vestido de pericón’‘yauhque, pericón; me, vestido’) en la mitología mexica es el dios de las asclepias, asteráceas, rosáceas, gutíferas y hipericáceas, identificado como uno de los cuatro Tlaloque.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Yauhqueme (bahasa Nahuatl: \"berpakaian pericón\") dalam mitologi Mexica adalah dewa berbagai tumbuhan seperti asclepias dan asteraceae, salah satu dari empat Tlaloque.",
        "en": "Yauhqueme (Nahuatl, \"dressed in pericón\") in Mexica mythology is the god of plants such as asclepias and asters, one of the four Tlaloque."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "yauhqueme-c02",
      "source_id": "yauhqueme-s1",
      "quote": "Y al oeste, Yauhqueme (el vestido de Yauhtli o pericón), en veneración del tlaloque dueño del maíz amarillo, resignificado en la figura del Cristo de Huayotenco o “señor del buen camino”, como “Señor de Tlacpac”.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di Acaxochitlán, Yauhqueme adalah tlaloque arah barat, pemilik jagung kuning, yang kini dihormati sebagai Kristus dari Huayotenco, \"Señor de Tlacpac\".",
        "en": "In Acaxochitlán, Yauhqueme is the tlaloque of the west, owner of yellow maize, now revered as the Christ of Huayotenco, \"Señor de Tlacpac\"."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "yauhqueme-c03",
      "source_id": "yauhqueme-s2",
      "quote": "в ацтекській міфології — помічник Тлалока; один із чотирьох тлалоке, відповідальних за розподіл дощу на землі.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam mitologi Aztek ia pembantu Tlaloc, salah satu dari empat tlaloque yang membagikan hujan di bumi.",
        "en": "In Aztec mythology he is a helper of Tlaloc, one of the four tlaloque who distribute rain on earth."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "yauhqueme-c04",
      "source_id": "yauhqueme-s2",
      "quote": "Його ім'я пов'язане з однойменним пагорбом поблизу Атлакуїхуая (теперішня Такубая), де богам дощу приносили в жертву дітей. Був також божеством Отомі.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Namanya terkait dengan bukit bernama sama dekat Atlacuihuayan (kini Tacubaya), tempat anak-anak dikorbankan bagi dewa-dewa hujan; ia juga dewa orang Otomi.",
        "en": "His name is linked to a hill of the same name near Atlacuihuayan (now Tacubaya), where children were sacrificed to the rain gods; he was also a deity of the Otomi."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "yauhqueme-c05",
      "source_id": "yauhqueme-s2",
      "quote": "Його одяг складався з паперового капелюха, шлейфа із пір'я, смужки паперу на грудях, сандалів. Однією рукою він тримав брязкальце, а іншою — щит.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pakaiannya terdiri atas topi kertas, juntaian bulu, pita kertas di dada, dan sandal; satu tangannya memegang kerincingan dan tangan lainnya perisai.",
        "en": "His attire was a paper hat, a feather train, a paper strip on the chest and sandals; in one hand he held a rattle and in the other a shield."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Zezengorri adalah roh gua dalam mitologi Basque yang keluar berwujud banteng merah dan menjaga gua serta harta.",
    "en": "Zezengorri is a cave spirit of Basque mythology that emerges as a red bull and guards caves and treasure.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "zezengorri-c01",
      "zezengorri-c04",
      "zezengorri-c05"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Zezengorri, \"banteng merah\", adalah roh mitologi Basque yang tinggal di gua dan keluar berwujud banteng merah; di beberapa daerah ia sapi betina merah bernama Behigorri. Banteng-banteng gua ini umumnya agresif dan menyerang atau menghalangi orang masuk. Seekor banteng api menjaga gua-gua Mari di Austokieta, dan banteng merah penyembur api menjaga harta di gua Atxulaur.",
      "en": "Zezengorri, \"red bull\", is a Basque spirit living in caves that emerges as a red bull; in some areas it is a red cow called Behigorri. These cave bulls are mostly aggressive, attacking people or barring their way. A fire bull guards the caves of Mari at Austokieta, and a fire-breathing red bull guards treasure in the cave of Atxulaur.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "zezengorri-c01",
        "zezengorri-c02",
        "zezengorri-c03",
        "zezengorri-c04",
        "zezengorri-c05"

    {
      "id": "Penelitian baru mengaitkan Zezengorri dan makhluk serupa dengan gua-gua yang memiliki gambar hewan yang sama dari zaman Paleolitik.",
      "en": "Recent studies link Zezengorri and similar beings to caves with Palaeolithic images of the same animals.",

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "zezengorri-c01",
      "source_id": "zezengorri-s2",
      "quote": "En la mitologia basca, Zezengorri ('toro vermell', del basc zezen 'toro' i gorri 'vermell') és un geni que viu en coves o altres cavitats subterrànies i que, en sortir-ne, adopta la forma de toro vermell.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam mitologi Basque, Zezengorri (\"banteng merah\", dari zezen \"banteng\" dan gorri \"merah\") adalah roh yang tinggal di gua atau rongga bawah tanah dan berwujud banteng merah saat keluar.",
        "en": "In Basque mythology Zezengorri ('red bull', from zezen 'bull' and gorri 'red') is a spirit living in caves or underground cavities that takes the form of a red bull when it comes out."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "zezengorri-c02",
      "source_id": "zezengorri-s2",
      "quote": "Segons la zona o la llegenda, en comptes d'un toro és una vaca vermella i aleshores se l'anomena Behigorri (behi és 'vaca' en basc).",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di beberapa daerah ia berupa sapi betina merah dan disebut Behigorri.",
        "en": "In some areas or legends it is a red cow instead, called Behigorri."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "zezengorri-c03",
      "source_id": "zezengorri-s1",
      "quote": "Les taureaux cavernicoles de la mythologie basque sont pour la plupart agressifs et peuvent attaquer et effrayer les gens dans la grotte ou leur refuser l'entrée.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Banteng-banteng gua dalam mitologi Basque umumnya agresif, dapat menyerang dan menakuti orang di gua atau melarang mereka masuk.",
        "en": "The cave bulls of Basque mythology are mostly aggressive and may attack and frighten people in the cave or refuse them entry."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "zezengorri-c04",
      "source_id": "zezengorri-s2",
      "quote": "Un toro de foc (o d'or, segons les variants) habita la cova d'Austokieta, a la muntanya d'Oxabi (Lizartza) i és el guardià de les coves de Mari.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Seekor banteng api (atau emas) tinggal di gua Austokieta di gunung Oxabi, Lizartza, dan menjaga gua-gua Mari.",
        "en": "A fire bull (or gold, in some versions) lives in the cave of Austokieta on Mount Oxabi (Lizartza) and guards the caves of Mari."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "zezengorri-c05",
      "source_id": "zezengorri-s2",
      "quote": "Un toro vermell que treu foc per la boca i el musell guarda un tresor a la cova d'Atxulaur (prop d'Orozko ).",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Seekor banteng merah yang menyemburkan api dari mulut dan moncongnya menjaga harta di gua Atxulaur dekat Orozko.",
        "en": "A red bull breathing fire from its mouth and muzzle guards a treasure in the cave of Atxulaur near Orozko."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "zezengorri-c06",
      "source_id": "zezengorri-s2",
      "quote": "Estudis recents han demostrat que tant Zezengorri, Behigorri i Aatxe com altres entitats mitològiques (Ihizi, els laminak), s'associen amb llocs on aquests mateixos animals van ser representats pels homes del Paleolític.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Penelitian baru menunjukkan Zezengorri, Behigorri, Aatxe, dan makhluk lain dikaitkan dengan tempat-tempat yang memiliki gambar hewan yang sama dari manusia Paleolitik.",
        "en": "Recent studies show that Zezengorri, Behigorri, Aatxe and other beings are linked to places where Palaeolithic people depicted those same animals."
      }
    }
  ],

    "id": "Abhartach adalah kerdil penyihir tiran dalam legenda Irlandia yang terus bangkit dari kubur sampai dikubur terbalik, sering dikaitkan dengan vampir.",
    "en": "Abhartach is a tyrant dwarf-magician of Irish legend who kept rising from the grave until buried head down, often linked with vampires.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "abhartach-c01",

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Abhartach, \"kerdil\" dalam bahasa Irlandia, adalah legenda Irlandia awal yang dicatat Patrick Weston Joyce pada 1870. Kerdil ini penyihir dan tiran kejam yang dibunuh kepala suku tetangga, menurut sebagian orang Fionn Mac Cumhail. Ia dikubur berdiri tetapi bangkit lagi keesokan harinya, lebih kejam dari sebelumnya. Atas nasihat seorang druid ia dibunuh untuk ketiga kalinya dan dikubur dengan kepala di bawah, sehingga tak pernah muncul lagi.",
      "en": "Abhartach, Irish for \"dwarf\", is an early Irish legend recorded by Patrick Weston Joyce in 1870. The dwarf was a magician and cruel tyrant slain by a neighbouring chieftain, some say Fionn Mac Cumhail. Buried standing, he rose again the next day crueller than ever. On a druid's advice he was killed a third time and buried head downwards, and never appeared again.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "abhartach-c01",
        "abhartach-c03",
        "abhartach-c04",
        "abhartach-c05"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Dalam versi modern ia bangkit untuk meminum darah rakyatnya dan hanya dapat ditahan dengan pedang kayu yew, dikubur terbalik di bawah duri dan batu besar. Sebagian sarjana menduga legenda ini mengilhami Dracula karya Bram Stoker.",
      "en": "In modern versions he rises to drink his subjects' blood and can only be restrained with a yew-wood sword and an upside-down burial under thorns and a great stone. Some scholars think the legend inspired Bram Stoker's Dracula.",
      "claim_ids": [

      "statement": {
        "id": "Abhartach (bahasa Irlandia: \"kerdil\"), juga Avartagh, adalah legenda Irlandia awal yang pertama kali dicatat dalam The Origin and History of Irish Names of Places karya Patrick Weston Joyce (1870).",
        "en": "Abhartach (Irish for 'dwarf'), also Avartagh, is an early Irish legend first collected in Patrick Weston Joyce's The Origin and History of Irish Names of Places (1870)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "abhartach-c02",
      "source_id": "abhartach-s1",
      "quote": "The central figure bears many striking similarities to later ideas of vampires. Some 21st-century scholars have theorised that the legend may have served as an inspiration for Irish author Bram Stoker in his creation of Dracula.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Tokoh utamanya sangat mirip gagasan vampir di kemudian hari, dan sebagian sarjana abad ke-21 menduga legenda ini mengilhami Bram Stoker menciptakan Dracula.",
        "en": "The central figure strikingly resembles later ideas of vampires, and some 21st-century scholars suggest the legend may have inspired Bram Stoker's Dracula."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "abhartach-c03",
      "source_id": "abhartach-s1",
      "quote": "This dwarf was a magician, and a dreadful tyrant, and after having perpetrated great cruelties on the people he was at last vanquished and slain by a neighbouring chieftain; some say by Fionn Mac Cumhail.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kerdil ini penyihir dan tiran mengerikan yang setelah berbuat sangat kejam akhirnya dikalahkan dan dibunuh kepala suku tetangga, menurut sebagian orang oleh Fionn Mac Cumhail.",
        "en": "The dwarf was a magician and dreadful tyrant who, after great cruelties, was finally defeated and slain by a neighbouring chieftain, some say by Fionn Mac Cumhail."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "abhartach-c04",
      "source_id": "abhartach-s1",
      "quote": "He was buried in a standing posture, but the very next day he appeared in his old haunts, more cruel and vigorous than ever.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia dikubur dalam posisi berdiri, tetapi keesokan harinya muncul lagi di tempat lamanya, lebih kejam dan kuat dari sebelumnya.",
        "en": "He was buried standing up, but the very next day appeared in his old haunts, crueller and stronger than ever."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "abhartach-c05",
      "source_id": "abhartach-s1",
      "quote": "The chief then consulted a druid, and according to his directions, he slew the dwarf a third time, and buried him in the same place, with his head downwards; which subdued his magical power, so that he never again appeared on earth.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Setelah ia lolos lagi dari kubur, kepala suku berkonsultasi dengan seorang druid, membunuhnya untuk ketiga kalinya, dan menguburnya dengan kepala di bawah, sehingga kekuatan sihirnya padam dan ia tak pernah muncul lagi.",
        "en": "After he escaped the grave again, the chief consulted a druid, slew him a third time and buried him head downwards, which subdued his magic so he never appeared again."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "abhartach-c06",
      "source_id": "abhartach-s1",
      "quote": "In some modern versions of the story Abhartach rises from his grave to drink the blood of his subjects, while the chieftain who slays the revenant is named as Cathain.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam beberapa versi modern Abhartach bangkit dari kubur untuk meminum darah rakyatnya, dan kepala suku yang membunuhnya bernama Cathain.",
        "en": "In some modern versions Abhartach rises from his grave to drink his subjects' blood, and the chieftain who slays him is named Cathain."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "abhartach-c07",
      "source_id": "abhartach-s1",
      "quote": "he can only be restrained by killing him with a sword made of yew wood, burying him upside down, surrounding his grave with thorns, and placing a large stone on top of the grave.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam versi itu ia hanya dapat ditahan dengan dibunuh memakai pedang kayu yew, dikubur terbalik, kuburnya dikelilingi duri, dan ditindih batu besar.",
        "en": "In those versions he can only be restrained by killing him with a yew-wood sword, burying him upside down, ringing the grave with thorns and placing a large stone on top."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],
  "gaps": [
    {
      "field": "sources",

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Alma de mestre adalah arwah nakhoda yang tewas karam dalam tradisi pelaut Portugal, yang ratapannya terdengar sebagai cicitan petrel badai.",
    "en": "The alma de mestre is the soul of a drowned ship's master in Portuguese seafaring tradition, whose laments are heard in the cries of the storm petrel.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "alma-de-mestre-c01",
      "alma-de-mestre-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Dalam tradisi nelayan dan pelaut Portugal, alma de mestre adalah arwah nakhoda atau kapten kapal yang tewas karam. Pelaut percaya cicitan sedih petrel badai adalah ratapan arwah-arwah ini, yang terus meratap selama jasad mereka belum sampai ke darat dan dimakamkan secara Kristen. Júlia Tomás mengaitkannya dengan mitos Ceyx dan Alkyone dalam Metamorphoses karya Ovidius.",
      "en": "In the traditions of Portuguese fishermen and sailors, an alma de mestre is the soul of a ship's master or captain lost at sea. Sailors believe the plaintive cries of the storm petrel are the laments of these souls, which mourn until their bodies reach land and receive Christian burial. Júlia Tomás links the belief with the myth of Ceyx and Alcyone in Ovid's Metamorphoses.",

  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "alma-de-mestre-c01",
      "source_id": "alma-de-mestre-s1",
      "quote": "Uma alma de mestre é, nas tradições dos pescadores e marinheiros de Portugal, a alma de um mestre ou capitão de um navio que se perdeu em naufrágio.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam tradisi nelayan dan pelaut Portugal, alma de mestre adalah arwah nakhoda atau kapten kapal yang tewas dalam kapal karam.",
        "en": "In the traditions of Portuguese fishermen and sailors, an alma de mestre is the soul of a ship's master or captain lost in a shipwreck."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "alma-de-mestre-c02",
      "source_id": "alma-de-mestre-s1",
      "quote": "A crença deve-se aos pios sentidos de certas aves, por antonomásia o Hydrobates pelagicus, que os marinheiros crêem serem os lamentos das almas dos mestres e capitães",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kepercayaan ini berasal dari cicitan sedih burung tertentu, terutama petrel badai (Hydrobates pelagicus), yang diyakini pelaut sebagai ratapan arwah para nakhoda dan kapten.",
        "en": "The belief comes from the plaintive cries of certain birds, above all the storm petrel (Hydrobates pelagicus), which sailors believe are the laments of the souls of masters and captains."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "alma-de-mestre-c03",
      "source_id": "alma-de-mestre-s1",
      "quote": "andam naquele fadário de pios, enquanto seu corpo não chega a terra, e não obtêm sepultura cristã.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mereka terus meratap selama jasadnya belum sampai ke darat dan belum dimakamkan secara Kristen.",
        "en": "They keep up their lament as long as their bodies have not reached land and received Christian burial."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "alma-de-mestre-c04",
      "source_id": "alma-de-mestre-s1",
      "quote": "Júlia Tomás, no seu «Ensaio sobre o Imaginário Marítimo dos Portugueses», propõe uma ligação entre este mito português e o mito grego, exarado nas Metamorfoses de Ovídio, de Ceix e Alcíone",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Júlia Tomás mengaitkan mitos ini dengan mitos Yunani Ceyx dan Alkyone dalam Metamorphoses karya Ovidius.",
        "en": "Júlia Tomás links this myth with the Greek myth of Ceyx and Alcyone in Ovid's Metamorphoses."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Avurie adalah roh malam dari Taranto berwujud kucing bertopi yang duduk di dada orang tidur hingga sesak napas.",
    "en": "The Avurie is a night spirit of Taranto in the form of a hatted cat that sits on sleepers' chests and takes their breath.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "avurie-c01",
      "avurie-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Avurie atau Aure adalah tokoh kepercayaan rakyat provinsi Taranto, terutama Massafra. Ia roh yang muncul pada malam hari menjelang fajar dalam wujud kucing bertopi, duduk di dada orang tidur hingga lumpuh dan sesak napas; di daerah lain ia berwujud anjing atau merpati. Ia juga suka mengepang rambut gadis dan surai kuda. Sebagian orang menganggapnya tafsir rakyat atas kelumpuhan tidur.",
      "en": "The Avurie or Aure is a figure of folk belief in the province of Taranto, especially Massafra. It is a spirit that appears at night towards dawn as a cat wearing a hat, sitting on sleepers' chests until they are paralysed and breathless; elsewhere it takes the form of a dog or dove. It also likes to braid girls' hair and horses' manes. Some see it as a folk interpretation of sleep paralysis.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "avurie-c01",
        "avurie-c02",
        "avurie-c03",
        "avurie-c04",
        "avurie-c06"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Siapa yang merebut topinya dapat menjadikannya budak dan memaksanya menunjukkan harta tersembunyi. Aure bisa terikat pada sebuah rumah atau mengikuti sebuah keluarga ke mana pun.",
      "en": "Whoever snatches its hat can enslave it and force it to reveal hidden treasure. An Aure may be bound to a house or follow a family wherever it goes.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "avurie-c05",
        "avurie-c07"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "cultural_context": null,
  "etymology": {
    "original_form": "augurium",
    "language": "Latin",
    "literal_meaning": {

      "published": null,
      "language": "it",

      "accessed": "2026-10-02"
    }
  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "avurie-c01",
      "source_id": "avurie-s1",
      "quote": "L'Avurje (anche Aure o Iura) è il protagonista di una credenza popolare molto diffusa nella provincia di Taranto, in particolare a Massafra e nel borgo antico di Taranto.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Avurje (juga Aure atau Iura) adalah tokoh kepercayaan rakyat yang tersebar luas di provinsi Taranto, terutama di Massafra dan kota tua Taranto.",
        "en": "The Avurje (also Aure or Iura) is the figure of a popular belief widespread in the province of Taranto, especially Massafra and the old town of Taranto."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "avurie-c02",
      "source_id": "avurie-s1",
      "quote": "Si tratta di un folletto o spirito che appare di notte, soprattutto verso l'alba, assumendo le sembianze di un gatto con un cappello, e si posa sul petto del malcapitato dormiente, paralizzandolo e togliendogli il respiro.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia peri atau roh yang muncul pada malam hari, terutama menjelang fajar, berwujud kucing bertopi, lalu duduk di dada orang tidur hingga lumpuh dan sesak napas.",
        "en": "It is a sprite or spirit that appears at night, especially towards dawn, in the form of a cat wearing a hat, settling on a sleeper's chest, paralysing them and taking their breath."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "avurie-c03",
      "source_id": "avurie-s1",
      "quote": "l'A(v)ùrje (s.m. lat. augurium) è definito uno spirito folletto che si aggira di notte per le case e si diverte a intrecciare i capelli delle ragazze nel sonno e le criniere dei cavalli.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut kamus dialek Massafra karya Roberto Caprara, A(v)ùrje (dari bahasa Latin augurium) adalah roh peri yang berkeliaran di rumah pada malam hari dan suka mengepang rambut gadis yang tidur dan surai kuda.",
        "en": "Roberto Caprara's dictionary of the Massafra dialect defines A(v)ùrje (from Latin augurium) as a sprite that roams houses at night and enjoys braiding sleeping girls' hair and horses' manes."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "avurie-c04",
      "source_id": "avurie-s1",
      "quote": "Altri, invece, riconducono la superstizione ad una interpretazione popolare della paralisi nel sonno.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sebagian orang menganggap takhayul ini tafsir rakyat atas kelumpuhan tidur.",
        "en": "Others trace the superstition to a folk interpretation of sleep paralysis."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "avurie-c05",
      "source_id": "avurie-s1",
      "quote": "La tradizione vuole che se si ha la prontezza di strappare il cappello all'Avurie, lo si renderà schiavo e lo si potrà costringere a rivelare il nascondiglio di un tesoro nascosto.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut tradisi, siapa yang sigap merebut topi Avurie akan menjadikannya budak dan dapat memaksanya menunjukkan tempat harta tersembunyi.",
        "en": "Tradition says that whoever is quick enough to snatch the Avurie's hat will enslave it and can force it to reveal a hidden treasure."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "avurie-c06",
      "source_id": "avurie-s1",
      "quote": "In altre zone della provincia si pensa abbia forma di cane o colombo.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di daerah lain provinsi itu ia dianggap berwujud anjing atau merpati.",
        "en": "In other parts of the province it is thought to take the form of a dog or a dove."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "avurie-c07",
      "source_id": "avurie-s1",
      "quote": "L'Aure può essere proprio di un'abitazione, ed allora non l'abbandonerà mai, o essere legato ad una famiglia, ed allora la seguirà nei suoi spostamenti.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Aure bisa terikat pada sebuah rumah dan tak pernah meninggalkannya, atau pada sebuah keluarga dan mengikutinya ke mana pun mereka pindah.",
        "en": "An Aure may belong to a house and never leave it, or be tied to a family and follow it wherever it moves."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],
  "gaps": [
    {
      "field": "sources",

    }
  ]
}

  "traits": [],
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Babau adalah monster khayalan berwujud tidak jelas dalam cerita rakyat Italia yang disebut untuk menakuti anak-anak.",
    "en": "The Babau is an imaginary monster of ill-defined shape in Italian folklore, invoked to frighten children.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "babau-c01"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Babau adalah monster khayalan dalam cerita rakyat Italia dan Eropa lain yang wujudnya tidak jelas dan biasa disebut untuk menakuti anak-anak (\"kalau tidak berhenti, kupanggil Babau!\"). Ia sangat berkaitan dengan uomo nero dan orc dalam dongeng.",
      "en": "The Babau is an imaginary monster of Italian and other European folklore, of ill-defined shape, traditionally invoked to scare children (\"stop it or I'll call the Babau!\"). It is closely related to the uomo nero and the ogre of fairy tales.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "babau-c01",
        "babau-c02"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Asal-usulnya tidak pasti: mungkin warisan ketakutan terhadap orang Saracen, dengan nama dari kata Arab Baban, atau lebih mungkin tiruan bunyi gonggongan. Dino Buzzati menulis cerita dan melukis tentangnya.",
      "en": "Its origin is uncertain: perhaps a legacy of fear of the Saracens, with a name from Arabic Baban, or more likely an imitation of barking. Dino Buzzati wrote a story about it and painted it.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "babau-c03",
        "babau-c04",
        "babau-c05"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "cultural_context": null,
  "etymology": null,
  "story_mode": {

  "did_you_know": null,

  "modern_depictions": [],
  "tradition_vs_modern": null,
  "learning_questions": [],

  "sources": [
    {
      "id": "babau-s1",
      "url": "https://it.wikipedia.org/wiki/Babau",
      "title": "Babau",
      "author": null,
      "publisher": "Wikipedia (it)",
      "published": null,
      "language": "it",
      "type": "wikipedia",
      "accessed": "2026-10-02"
    }
  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "babau-c01",
      "source_id": "babau-s1",
      "quote": "Il Babau (più raramente Babao, Bobò, Barabao, Maramao, Baubau, Bau Bau, Mamau, Maumau o Mau Mau) è, nel folclore italiano e in altri folclori europei, un mostro immaginario dalle caratteristiche non ben definite che viene tradizionalmente evocato per spaventare i bambini",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Babau (juga Babao, Bobò, Baubau, Mau Mau, dan lain-lain) dalam cerita rakyat Italia dan Eropa lain adalah monster khayalan berwujud tidak jelas yang biasa disebut untuk menakuti anak-anak.",
        "en": "The Babau (also Babao, Bobò, Baubau, Mau Mau and others) is, in Italian and other European folklore, an imaginary monster of ill-defined features traditionally invoked to frighten children."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "babau-c02",
      "source_id": "babau-s1",
      "quote": "Si può intendere il Babau come strettamente correlato all'uomo nero e all'orco delle fiabe, o addirittura usare queste diverse denominazioni come sinonimi.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Babau sangat berkaitan dengan uomo nero (\"lelaki hitam\") dan orc dalam dongeng, bahkan nama-nama itu bisa dipakai bergantian.",
        "en": "The Babau is closely related to the uomo nero and the ogre of fairy tales, and the names can even be used interchangeably."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "babau-c03",
      "source_id": "babau-s1",
      "quote": "Secondo alcune fonti, il Babau potrebbe essere un retaggio dell'antico timore nei confronti dei Saraceni (IX-X secolo). In questo caso, la parola \"Babau\" potrebbe derivare dall'arabo Baban (ossia \"Vincitore\").",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut sebagian sumber, Babau mungkin warisan ketakutan lama terhadap orang Saracen (abad ke-9 sampai ke-10), dan namanya mungkin berasal dari kata Arab Baban, \"pemenang\".",
        "en": "Some sources say the Babau may be a legacy of the old fear of the Saracens (9th–10th centuries), its name perhaps from Arabic Baban, \"victor\"."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "babau-c04",
      "source_id": "babau-s1",
      "quote": "Un'interpretazione più diffusa intende invece il nome \"Babau\" come onomatopea, ottenuto per raddoppiamento dal latrato del cane o di un altro animale.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Tafsir yang lebih umum menganggap Babau tiruan bunyi, dari pengulangan gonggongan anjing atau suara hewan lain.",
        "en": "A more common view takes the name as onomatopoeic, a doubling of a dog's bark or another animal's cry."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "babau-c05",
      "source_id": "babau-s1",
      "quote": "Al Babau è dedicato il racconto omonimo di Dino Buzzati (che lo ritrasse anche in diversi dipinti).",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dino Buzzati menulis cerita pendek berjudul Babau dan juga melukisnya dalam beberapa lukisan.",
        "en": "Dino Buzzati wrote a short story titled after the Babau and also painted it several times."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [
    {
      "topic": {
        "id": "Asal nama Babau",
        "en": "Origin of the name Babau"
      },

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Babieca adalah hewan mitos yang suaranya di atap pada malam hari mengabarkan kematian.",
    "en": "The babieca is a mythical animal whose call on the roof at night announces a death.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "babieca-c01"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Babieca adalah hewan mitologis yang dikaitkan dengan kematian. Bila ia bersuara ia mengabarkan musibah, dan bila suaranya terdengar di atap pada malam hari, seseorang di rumah itu diyakini akan segera meninggal.",
      "en": "The babieca is a mythological animal associated with death. Its song announces misfortune, and if it is heard on the roof at night someone in that house is believed to be about to die.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "babieca-c01"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "cultural_context": null,
  "etymology": null,
  "story_mode": {

    },

      "quote": "A babieca ye un animal mitolochico que gosa asociar-se a la muerte. Cuan canta anuncia una desgracia, por eso se diz a sobén que si se siente de nueiz en o tellau significa que luego morirá una persona d'ixa casa.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Babieca adalah hewan mitologis yang sering dikaitkan dengan kematian; bila ia bersuara ia mengabarkan musibah, sehingga orang sering berkata bila suaranya terdengar di atap pada malam hari, seseorang di rumah itu akan segera meninggal.",
        "en": "The babieca is a mythological animal often associated with death; when it sings it announces misfortune, so it is often said that if it is heard on the roof at night someone in that house will soon die."

    {
      "id": "badalischio-c01",
      "source_id": "badalischio-s1",
      "quote": "Il badalischio è una tipica leggenda della valle del Casentino (Toscana, Provincia di Arezzo).",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Badalischio adalah legenda khas lembah Casentino (Toskana, Provinsi Arezzo).",
        "en": "The badalischio is a typical legend of the Casentino valley (Tuscany, Province of Arezzo)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "badalischio-c02",
      "source_id": "badalischio-s1",
      "quote": "Si racconta che questo mostro (simile al basilisco, altra creatura mitologica) sia nato nella Gorga Nera, un piccolo laghetto in prossimità della Fonte del Borbotto (Parco Nazionale delle Foreste Casentinesi).",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Monster ini, mirip basilisk, konon lahir di Gorga Nera, danau kecil dekat Fonte del Borbotto di Taman Nasional Hutan Casentino.",
        "en": "This monster, similar to the basilisk, is said to have been born in the Gorga Nera, a small lake near the Fonte del Borbotto in the Casentino Forests National Park."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "badalischio-c03",
      "source_id": "badalischio-s1",
      "quote": "uno strano serpente, grande come un uomo, avvolto in bende di lino da neonato, con occhi rossi in grado di paralizzare la malcapitata preda.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia ular aneh sebesar manusia, terbungkus kain lampin linen bayi, dengan mata merah yang dapat melumpuhkan mangsanya.",
        "en": "It is a strange serpent as big as a man, wrapped in linen swaddling bands, with red eyes able to paralyse its prey."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "badalischio-c04",
      "source_id": "badalischio-s1",
      "quote": "Alcune leggende affermano che l'alito del badalischio sia estremamente velenoso, addirittura mortale. Il badalischio viene spesso raffigurato con una corona od un diadema, che a volte gli copre gli occhi.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sebagian legenda menyebut napasnya sangat beracun bahkan mematikan, dan ia sering digambarkan bermahkota atau berdiadem yang kadang menutupi matanya.",
        "en": "Some legends say its breath is extremely poisonous, even deadly, and it is often shown with a crown or diadem that sometimes covers its eyes."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "badalischio-c05",
      "source_id": "badalischio-s1",
      "quote": "Il giornale Casentino 2000 ha creato un premio satirico da offrire a certe persone in particolare (un premio simile al Tapiro d'oro di Striscia la notizia), chiamato \"Il Badalischio d'Argento\".",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Surat kabar Casentino 2000 membuat penghargaan satir bernama \"Il Badalischio d'Argento\".",
        "en": "The newspaper Casentino 2000 created a satirical award called \"Il Badalischio d'Argento\"."
      }
    }

  ],
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Balborinho adalah pusaran angin dalam cerita rakyat Portugal yang membawa arwah petani pencuri tanah, iblis, atau penyihir.",
    "en": "The balborinho is a whirlwind of Portuguese folklore carrying the souls of land-stealing peasants, devils or witches.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "balborinho-c01",
      "balborinho-c02",
      "balborinho-c03"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Balborinho adalah pusaran angin dalam cerita rakyat Portugal yang muncul pada \"jam terbuka\", terutama di persimpangan jalan. Di dalamnya berputar arwah petani yang mencuri tanah dan tak bisa masuk surga sampai mengembalikan curiannya. Pada abad ke-19 orang juga percaya iblis dan penyihir muncul di dalamnya.",
      "en": "The balborinho is a whirlwind of Portuguese folklore that appears at the \"open hours\", mainly at crossroads. Within it spin the souls of peasants who stole land and cannot enter heaven until they return what they took. In the 19th century people also believed devils and witches appeared inside it.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "balborinho-c01",
        "balborinho-c02",
        "balborinho-c03"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Cara menangkalnya berbeda di tiap daerah: di Briteiros orang membuat tanda salib sambil mengucapkan doa, di Moncorvo orang melemparkan pisau lipat ke tengah pusaran untuk mengusir penyihir, dan di Guimarães setiap daun yang terbawa dianggap membawa iblis.",
      "en": "Remedies varied by place: in Briteiros people crossed themselves and said a formula, in Moncorvo they threw a penknife into the whirlwind to drive out the witch, and in Guimarães each leaf it carried was thought to bear a devil.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "balborinho-c04",
        "balborinho-c05",
        "balborinho-c06"
      ]
    }
  ],

      "published": null,

    }
  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "balborinho-c01",
      "source_id": "balborinho-s1",
      "quote": "O balborinho (grafias alternativas borborinho, barborinho ou belborinho) é um ser mítico do folclore português. O balborinho é um redemoinho de vento que surge nas horas abertas, principalmente nas encruzilhadas dos caminhos.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Balborinho (juga borborinho, barborinho, atau belborinho) adalah makhluk mitos cerita rakyat Portugal, pusaran angin yang muncul pada \"jam terbuka\", terutama di persimpangan jalan.",
        "en": "The balborinho (also borborinho, barborinho or belborinho) is a mythical being of Portuguese folklore, a whirlwind that appears at the \"open hours\", mainly at crossroads."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "balborinho-c02",
      "source_id": "balborinho-s1",
      "quote": "Dentro do balborinho vão as almas penadas de campesinos que cometeram delitos agrários, e não podem entrar no céu por estarem em dívida para com os vivos, tendo necessidade de lhes restituir o que surripiaram.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di dalam balborinho berputar arwah gentayangan petani yang melakukan kejahatan atas tanah dan tidak bisa masuk surga karena masih berutang kepada orang hidup dan harus mengembalikan apa yang mereka curi.",
        "en": "Inside the balborinho travel the lost souls of peasants who committed agrarian crimes and cannot enter heaven because they owe the living and must return what they stole."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "balborinho-c03",
      "source_id": "balborinho-s1",
      "quote": "Em várias zonas do país, no século XIX, havia a crença de que no seio dos balborinhos surgem diabos e bruxas.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada abad ke-19, di berbagai daerah orang percaya bahwa di dalam balborinho muncul iblis dan penyihir.",
        "en": "In the 19th century, many parts of the country believed devils and witches appeared within the balborinhos."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "balborinho-c04",
      "source_id": "balborinho-s1",
      "quote": "Em Briteiros, a recomendação que se fazia era persignar-se, enquanto se lhes dizia a seguinte invocação: «Vai-te para quem te comeu as leiras».",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di Briteiros orang membuat tanda salib sambil berkata \"Pergilah kepada orang yang memakan ladangmu\".",
        "en": "In Briteiros people crossed themselves while saying, \"Go to whoever ate your fields\"."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "balborinho-c05",
      "source_id": "balborinho-s1",
      "quote": "Em Moncorvo, por sua vez, recomendava-se atirar um canivete aberto ao centro do redemoinho de vento, a fim de esmadrigar de dentro do balborinho a Feiticeira ou Bruxa, que nele se ocultava.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di Moncorvo orang melemparkan pisau lipat terbuka ke tengah pusaran untuk mengusir penyihir yang bersembunyi di dalamnya.",
        "en": "In Moncorvo people threw an open penknife into the centre of the whirlwind to drive out the witch hiding inside."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "balborinho-c06",
      "source_id": "balborinho-s1",
      "quote": "Em Guimarães era crendice popular que em cada folha arrebatada ao vento de um balborinho ia um diabo.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di Guimarães orang percaya setiap daun yang terbawa angin balborinho membawa seorang iblis.",
        "en": "In Guimarães it was believed a devil rode on every leaf the balborinho's wind snatched up."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Baldemora adalah putri duyung dalam mitologi Katalan yang memandu pelaut yang tersesat di laut buruk, kecuali pada hari Santo Petrus.",
    "en": "The baldemora is a mermaid of Catalan mythology who guides sailors lost in rough seas, except on Saint Peter's day.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "baldemora-c01",
      "baldemora-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Baldemora adalah semacam putri duyung dalam mitologi Katalan, berambut panjang keemasan atau keperakan dan bergerak sangat cepat. Ia tinggal di laut dan memandu pelaut yang tersesat karena laut buruk. Karena ia merayakan pestanya pada hari Santo Petrus, pada hari itu sebaiknya tidak melaut.",
      "en": "The baldemora is a kind of mermaid of Catalan mythology with long golden or silver hair that moves very fast. It lives in the sea and guides sailors lost in rough seas. Since it holds its feast on Saint Peter's day, it is best not to sail on that day.",

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "baldemora-c01",
      "source_id": "baldemora-s1",
      "quote": "Una baldemora és, dins la mitologia catalana, una mena de sirena amb una llarga cabellera, a voltes daurada, a voltes platejada que es mou amb gran rapidesa. Viu dins de la mar i ajuda a guiar els mariners quan aquests han perdut el nord per culpa de la mala maror.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam mitologi Katalan, baldemora adalah semacam putri duyung berambut panjang, kadang keemasan kadang keperakan, yang bergerak sangat cepat; ia tinggal di laut dan membantu memandu pelaut yang tersesat karena laut buruk.",
        "en": "In Catalan mythology a baldemora is a kind of mermaid with long hair, sometimes golden, sometimes silver, that moves very fast; it lives in the sea and helps guide sailors who have lost their way in rough seas."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "baldemora-c02",
      "source_id": "baldemora-s1",
      "quote": "Com que celebra la seva festa per Sant Pere, aquest dia el millor és no fer-se a la mar, perquè la baldemora no vetllarà per la bona marxa de les naus.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Karena ia merayakan pestanya pada hari Santo Petrus, pada hari itu sebaiknya tidak melaut, sebab baldemora tidak akan menjaga perjalanan kapal.",
        "en": "Because it holds its feast on Saint Peter's day, it is best not to go to sea that day, since the baldemora will not watch over the ships."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],

    "id": "Banya Verda adalah iblis bertanduk hijau penakut anak dari cerita rakyat Katalan yang membawa anak nakal ke neraka dalam keranjang.",
    "en": "Banya Verda is a green-horned child-scaring devil of Catalan folklore who carries naughty children to hell in a basket.",
    "claim_ids": [

    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Banya Verda adalah momok penakut anak imajinasi rakyat Katalan, iblis dengan tanduk hijau besar di tengah dahi. Ia menusuk anak nakal dengan garpu tala, memasukkan mereka ke keranjang, dan membawa mereka ke neraka. Ia terkenal di Cerdanya dan juga di Mallorca, tempat ia tampil dalam tarian cossiers di Montuïri.",
      "en": "Banya Verda is a child-scaring bogey of Catalan folk imagination, a devil with a big green horn in the middle of its forehead. It pricks naughty children with a pitchfork, loads them in a basket and takes them to hell. It is known in the Cerdanya and also in Mallorca, where it appears in the cossiers dances of Montuïri.",

      "id": "banya-verda-c01",
      "source_id": "banya-verda-s1",
      "quote": "En Banya Verda és un espantamainades propi de l'imaginari popular català. Es tracta d'un dimoni que té una gran banya verda al mig del front. Duu una forca amb què punxa tots els nens dolents que troba, se'ls carrega en una cistella i se'ls enduu de pet a l'infern.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Banya Verda (\"tanduk hijau\") adalah momok penakut anak imajinasi rakyat Katalan, iblis bertanduk hijau besar di tengah dahi yang menusuk anak nakal dengan garpu tala, memasukkan mereka ke keranjang, dan membawa mereka langsung ke neraka.",
        "en": "Banya Verda (\"green horn\") is a child-scaring bogey of Catalan folk imagination, a devil with a big green horn in the middle of its forehead that pricks naughty children with a pitchfork, loads them in a basket and takes them straight to hell."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "banya-verda-c02",
      "source_id": "banya-verda-s1",
      "quote": "El personatge és especialment conegut a la Cerdanya, però també a Mallorca, on és un dels protagonistes dels balls tradicionals dels cossiers de Montuïri.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Tokoh ini terkenal terutama di Cerdanya, tetapi juga di Mallorca, tempat ia menjadi salah satu tokoh tarian tradisional cossiers di Montuïri.",
        "en": "The figure is especially known in the Cerdanya, but also in Mallorca, where it is one of the protagonists of the traditional cossiers dances of Montuïri."
      }
    }
  ],

    {
      "id": "Monster Banyoles atau Drac de Banyoles adalah naga legendaris dari danau Banyoles di Katalonia. Ia bersarang di gua La Draga, dapat terbang, berenang, dan berjalan, napasnya mencemari seluruh Gironès, dan ia memangsa hewan serta manusia. Charlemagne gagal membunuhnya karena pedangnya Joyeuse hancur pada sisiknya.",
      "en": "The Banyoles monster or Drac de Banyoles is a legendary dragon of the lake of Banyoles in Catalonia. It lived in the cave of La Draga, could fly, swim and walk, polluted the whole Gironès with its breath and devoured animals and people. Charlemagne failed to kill it when his sword Joyeuse shattered on its scales.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "banyoles-monster-c01",
        "banyoles-monster-c04",
        "banyoles-monster-c05",
        "banyoles-monster-c06"

    {
      "id": "Pada abad ke-8 biarawan Santo Emeterio (Sant Mer) menjinakkannya dengan doa. Menurut satu versi ia dibawa ke kota dan dibunuh warga; menurut versi lain ia diubah menjadi pemakan tumbuhan yang damai dan masih hidup di dasar danau. Legenda ini mirip kisah Tarasque.",
      "en": "In the 8th century the monk St Emeterio (Sant Mer) tamed it with prayers. In one version it was led into town and killed by the people; in another it was turned into a peaceful herbivore and still lives in the depths of the lake. The legend resembles that of the Tarasque.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "banyoles-monster-c02",
        "banyoles-monster-c07",
        "banyoles-monster-c08"
      ]
    },

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "banyoles-monster-c01",
      "source_id": "banyoles-monster-s1",
      "quote": "The Banyoles monster is a legendary monster, possibly a dragon, from the Lake of Banyoles in Catalonia.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Monster Banyoles adalah monster legendaris, mungkin seekor naga, dari danau Banyoles di Katalonia.",
        "en": "The Banyoles monster is a legendary monster, possibly a dragon, from the lake of Banyoles in Catalonia."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "banyoles-monster-c02",
      "source_id": "banyoles-monster-s1",
      "quote": "In the eighth century, a French monk named St. Emeterio coaxed the beast from the lake using prayers and transformed the creature into a peaceful herbivore. It is said the Banyoles Monster still lives in the depth of the lake today.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada abad ke-8, seorang biarawan Prancis bernama Santo Emeterio membujuk binatang itu keluar dari danau dengan doa dan mengubahnya menjadi pemakan tumbuhan yang damai; konon ia masih hidup di dasar danau sampai kini.",
        "en": "In the 8th century a French monk named St Emeterio coaxed the beast from the lake with prayers and turned it into a peaceful herbivore; it is said to live in the depths of the lake still."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "banyoles-monster-c03",
      "source_id": "banyoles-monster-s1",
      "quote": "The monster is the subject of a 2008 children's song \"El Monstre De Banyoles\" by Toni Jiménez.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Monster ini menjadi subjek lagu anak \"El Monstre De Banyoles\" karya Toni Jiménez tahun 2008.",
        "en": "The monster is the subject of the 2008 children's song \"El Monstre De Banyoles\" by Toni Jiménez."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "banyoles-monster-c04",
      "source_id": "banyoles-monster-s2",
      "quote": "el drac, enorme i ferotge, tenia el cau a l'estany de Banyoles, concretament, a la cova de la Draga",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel Katalan tentang Drac de Banyoles, naga yang besar dan buas itu bersarang di danau Banyoles, di gua La Draga.",
        "en": "According to the Catalan article on the Drac de Banyoles, the huge, fierce dragon had its lair in the lake of Banyoles, in the cave of La Draga."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "banyoles-monster-c05",
      "source_id": "banyoles-monster-s2",
      "quote": "Podia volar, nedar i caminar i amb el seu alè fètid infestava tot el Gironès. Era el terror de la zona i es menjava molts animals i persones",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia dapat terbang, berenang, dan berjalan, napasnya yang busuk mencemari seluruh Gironès, dan ia memangsa banyak hewan dan manusia.",
        "en": "It could fly, swim and walk, its fetid breath polluted the whole Gironès, and it devoured many animals and people."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "banyoles-monster-c06",
      "source_id": "banyoles-monster-s2",
      "quote": "Carlemany va atacar el drac brandant la seva mítica espasa Joyeuse, que tenia el do de no poder ser vençuda mai en batalla. Tanmateix, en intentar ferir el monstre, l'espasa es va trencar en mil bocins sense poder traspassar les seves dures escates.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Charlemagne menyerang naga itu dengan pedang mitisnya Joyeuse yang tak terkalahkan, tetapi pedang itu hancur tanpa menembus sisik kerasnya.",
        "en": "Charlemagne attacked the dragon with his legendary sword Joyeuse, which could never be beaten, but the sword shattered without piercing its hard scales."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "banyoles-monster-c07",
      "source_id": "banyoles-monster-s2",
      "quote": "El malson dels banyolins no es va acabar fins que Sant Mer va pacificar el drac i el va portar a la vila, on els ciutadans el van matar",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mimpi buruk warga Banyoles baru berakhir ketika Sant Mer (Emeterio) menjinakkan naga itu dan membawanya ke kota, tempat warga membunuhnya.",
        "en": "The nightmare ended only when Sant Mer (Emeterio) tamed the dragon and led it into town, where the citizens killed it."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "banyoles-monster-c08",
      "source_id": "banyoles-monster-s2",
      "quote": "Com es pot comprovar, la llegenda té una gran similitud amb la de la Tarasca de Tarascó",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Legenda ini sangat mirip legenda Tarasque dari Tarascon.",
        "en": "The legend closely resembles that of the Tarasque of Tarascon."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "banyoles-monster-c09",
      "source_id": "banyoles-monster-s2",
      "quote": "La figura del drac ha esdevingut una icona de Banyoles i nombroses entitats esportives de la ciutat",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Naga ini menjadi ikon Banyoles dan dipakai banyak klub olahraga kota itu pada lambangnya.",
        "en": "The dragon has become an icon of Banyoles, used by many of the town's sports clubs in their emblems."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [
    {
      "topic": {
        "id": "Akhir kisah naga",
        "en": "The dragon's fate"
      },
      "positions": [
        {
          "summary": {
            "id": "Dibunuh warga setelah dijinakkan.",

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Basilisc dalam mitologi Katalan adalah makhluk kecil penjaga harta di makam yang tatapannya mematikan.",
    "en": "In Catalan mythology the basilisc is a small treasure-guarding creature of tombs whose gaze kills.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "basilisc-c01",
      "basilisc-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Dalam mitologi Katalan, basilisc adalah makhluk kecil yang wujudnya berbeda menurut daerah, misalnya ular kecil, salamander, atau katak pohon. Ia tinggal di makam berisi benda berharga atau di tempat harta terkubur untuk mencegah pencurian, dan tatapannya mematikan. Pemburu harta membawa cermin, karena basilisc mati bila melihat bayangannya sendiri. Ia konon lahir dari telur ayam jantan atau dari uap emas.",
      "en": "In Catalan mythology the basilisc is a small being whose form varies by area, such as a little snake, a salamander or a tree frog. It lives in tombs with valuables or where treasure is buried, to prevent theft, and its gaze kills. Treasure hunters carried mirrors, since a basilisc dies on seeing its own reflection. It was said to hatch from cockerels' eggs or from the vapour of gold.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "basilisc-c01",
        "basilisc-c02",
        "basilisc-c03",
        "basilisc-c04"
      ]
    }
  ],

    {
      "id": "basilisc-s1",
      "url": "https://ca.wikipedia.org/wiki/Basilisc_(mitologia_catalana)",
      "title": "Basilisc (mitologia catalana)",
      "author": null,

      "published": null,
      "language": "ca",

    }
  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "basilisc-c01",
      "source_id": "basilisc-s1",
      "quote": "Un basilisc és, dins la mitologia catalana, una mena de petit ésser que es pot presentar en diverses formes segons la zona geogràfica, ja siguin animals (una petita serp, una salamandra o sargantana, una reineta) o indefinides.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam mitologi Katalan, basilisc adalah makhluk kecil yang wujudnya berbeda menurut daerah, berupa hewan seperti ular kecil, salamander atau kadal, katak pohon, atau tak berbentuk jelas.",
        "en": "In Catalan mythology a basilisc is a small being that takes various forms depending on the area, animal (a little snake, a salamander or lizard, a tree frog) or indefinite."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "basilisc-c02",
      "source_id": "basilisc-s1",
      "quote": "Viu dins els sepulcres on el finat fou enterrat amb objectes valuosos o en llocs on hi ha tresors enterrats, amb la missió d'impedir que ningú no els robi. La seva mirada és mortal, ja sigui directament o per mitjà d'un verí que en surt.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia tinggal di makam yang berisi benda berharga atau di tempat harta terkubur untuk mencegah pencurian, dan tatapannya mematikan, langsung atau lewat racun yang keluar darinya.",
        "en": "It lives in tombs where the dead were buried with valuables, or where treasure is buried, to stop anyone stealing it; its gaze is deadly, directly or through a poison it gives off."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "basilisc-c03",
      "source_id": "basilisc-s1",
      "quote": "Els cercadors de tresors anaven antigament equipats amb un mirall per tal d'esquivar el perill del basilisc, ja que aquest, en veure-s'hi reflectit, es moria víctima de la seva pròpia mirada.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pemburu harta dahulu membawa cermin untuk menghindarinya, karena basilisc yang melihat bayangannya sendiri akan mati oleh tatapannya.",
        "en": "Treasure hunters used to carry a mirror to avoid the basilisc, since on seeing its reflection it died of its own gaze."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "basilisc-c04",
      "source_id": "basilisc-s1",
      "quote": "els basiliscs podien néixer o bé d'uns petits ous de color verd, vermell o blau que ponien els galls, o bé del baf de l'or, ja que aquest metall contenia els gens de la bestiola.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Basilisc konon lahir dari telur kecil hijau, merah, atau biru yang ditelurkan ayam jantan, atau dari uap emas, karena logam itu mengandung benihnya.",
        "en": "Basiliscs were said to hatch from small green, red or blue eggs laid by cockerels, or from the vapour of gold, since that metal held the creature's seed."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Beerwolf adalah manusia serigala dalam dongeng Jerman, yang namanya dipakai Martin Luther pada 1539 untuk menyebut penguasa tiran yang boleh dilawan.",
    "en": "The Beerwolf is the werewolf of German folk tales, whose name Martin Luther used in 1539 for tyrannical rulers who may be resisted.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "beerwolf-c01",
      "beerwolf-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Beerwolf, Bärwolf, atau Werwolf adalah monster dongeng Jerman yang umumnya dikenal sebagai manusia serigala. Pada 1539, saat Kaisar Charles V tampak bersiap menyerang kaum Lutheran, Martin Luther memakai konsep Beerwolf untuk menggambarkan Paus dan Kaisar: berbeda dari tiran biasa, Beerwolf menjungkirbalikkan seluruh tatanan moral. Pengakuan Magdeburg tahun 1550 memuat klausul Beerwolf sebagai syarat melawan penguasa jahat.",
      "en": "The Beerwolf (also Bärwolf or Werwolf) is a monster of German folk tales, usually understood as a werewolf. In 1539, when Emperor Charles V seemed ready to attack the Lutherans, Martin Luther used the concept of the Beerwolf for the Pope and the Emperor: unlike a mere tyrant, the Beerwolf overturns the entire moral order. The 1550 Magdeburg Confession included a Beerwolf clause as a condition for resisting an evil ruler.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "beerwolf-c01",
        "beerwolf-c02",
        "beerwolf-c03",
        "beerwolf-c04"
      ]

        "id": "Beerwolf (Bärwolf, Werwolf) adalah monster dongeng Jerman yang umumnya dikenal sebagai manusia serigala.",

      "source_id": "beerwolf-s1",
      "quote": "In a debate arranged by Philip of Hesse and the Elector of Saxony in 1539, when it appeared the Holy Roman Emperor Charles V was readying to attack the Lutherans, Martin Luther introduced the concept of Beerwolf to describe the Pope and the Emperor.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam debat yang diadakan Philip dari Hesse dan Elektor Sachsen pada 1539, saat Kaisar Romawi Suci Charles V tampak bersiap menyerang kaum Lutheran, Martin Luther memakai konsep Beerwolf untuk menggambarkan Paus dan Kaisar.",
        "en": "In a 1539 debate arranged by Philip of Hesse and the Elector of Saxony, when Holy Roman Emperor Charles V seemed ready to attack the Lutherans, Martin Luther used the concept of the Beerwolf to describe the Pope and the Emperor."

      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam teori perlawanan, Beerwolf berbeda dari tiran biasa karena bukan hanya melanggar hukum tetapi menjungkirbalikkan seluruh tatanan moral yang mendasarinya.",
        "en": "In resistance theory the Beerwolf, unlike a mere tyrant, not only broke the law but overturned the whole moral order on which it rests."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "beerwolf-c04",
      "source_id": "beerwolf-s1",
      "quote": "The 1550 Magdeburg Confession included a Beerwolf clause that had to be fulfilled before an evil ruler could be resisted by the lesser magistrates.",

      "statement": {

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Bep-Kororoti adalah guru langit dalam legenda Kayapó yang konon datang berpakaian berkilau, mendirikan sekolah pertama, lalu lenyap di tengah guntur.",
    "en": "Bep-Kororoti is a sky teacher of Kayapó legend, said to have come in a shining suit, founded the first school and vanished amid thunder.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "bep-kororoti-c01",
      "bep-kororoti-c04",

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Bep-Kororoti adalah guru mitis dalam legenda Kayapó yang dicatat dalam bahasa Portugis oleh João Américo Peret. Ia datang ke desa leluhur Kayapó dengan pakaian berkilau; senjata para pejuang memantul darinya. Namanya dapat diartikan \"aku datang dari langit\", dan ia mendirikan sekolah pertama di hutan hujan. Bertahun-tahun kemudian ia mendaki bukit Pukato-Ti dan lenyap di tengah asap dan guntur.",
      "en": "Bep-Kororoti is a mythical teacher of Kayapó legend, recorded in Portuguese by João Américo Peret. He came to the Kayapó ancestors' village in a shining suit; the warriors' weapons bounced off him. His name is rendered \"I came from the sky\", and he founded the first school in the rainforest. Years later he climbed the hill of Pukato-Ti and vanished amid smoke and thunder.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "bep-kororoti-c01",
        "bep-kororoti-c02",
        "bep-kororoti-c03",
        "bep-kororoti-c04",
        "bep-kororoti-c05"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Dalam perayaan tahunan, seorang anggota suku dibalut pakaian jerami menurut pakaian Bep-Kororoti. Pakaian ini sering ditafsirkan sebagai pakaian antariksa, tetapi juga mirip pakaian peternak lebah.",
      "en": "At an annual celebration a tribesman is dressed in a straw costume modelled on Bep-Kororoti's suit. The costume is often read as a spacesuit, but it also resembles a beekeeper's suit.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "bep-kororoti-c06",

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "bep-kororoti-c01",
      "source_id": "bep-kororoti-s1",
      "quote": "Bep-Kororoti é um mítico professor que os índios Kayapó acreditam ter visitado seus ancestrais séculos atrás, vindo do espaço vestido com um terno brilhante, deu-lhes muito conhecimento e fundou a primeira escola.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel ini, Bep-Kororoti adalah guru mitis yang diyakini orang Kayapó pernah mengunjungi leluhur mereka berabad-abad lalu, datang dari angkasa dengan pakaian berkilau, memberi banyak pengetahuan, dan mendirikan sekolah pertama.",
        "en": "According to the article, Bep-Kororoti is a mythical teacher the Kayapó believe visited their ancestors centuries ago, coming from the sky in a shining suit, giving them much knowledge and founding the first school."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bep-kororoti-c02",
      "source_id": "bep-kororoti-s1",
      "quote": "O Dr. João Américo Peret, eminente pesquisador brasileiro da cultura indígena, foi o primeiro a registrar no idioma português a antiga lenda dos índios Kavapo sobre a criação do mundo e o inusitado mestre celeste Bep-Kororoti.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "João Américo Peret adalah orang pertama yang mencatat dalam bahasa Portugis legenda Kayapó tentang penciptaan dunia dan guru langit Bep-Kororoti.",
        "en": "João Américo Peret was the first to record in Portuguese the Kayapó legend of the creation of the world and the sky teacher Bep-Kororoti."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bep-kororoti-c03",
      "source_id": "bep-kororoti-s1",
      "quote": "Para seu total espanto, as flechas envenenadas, lanças e clavas simplesmente ricochetearam nele sem feri-lo.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam legenda itu, para pejuang menyerang orang asing berpakaian berkilau ini, tetapi panah beracun, tombak, dan gada memantul darinya tanpa melukai.",
        "en": "In the legend warriors attacked the stranger in the shining suit, but poisoned arrows, spears and clubs bounced off him without harm."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bep-kororoti-c04",
      "source_id": "bep-kororoti-s1",
      "quote": "A tradição diz que esse pacífico recém-chegado foi chamado Bep-Kororoti, que pode ser traduzido descritivamente como \"eu vim do céu\" ou \"eu vim do espaço\". Ele logo fundou a primeira escola - um lar para jovens - na floresta tropical",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pendatang damai itu disebut Bep-Kororoti, yang dapat diartikan \"aku datang dari langit\", dan ia mendirikan sekolah pertama, rumah bagi kaum muda, di hutan hujan.",
        "en": "The peaceful newcomer was called Bep-Kororoti, rendered as \"I came from the sky\", and he founded the first school, a house for young men, in the rainforest."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bep-kororoti-c05",
      "source_id": "bep-kororoti-s1",
      "quote": "Depois que Bep-Kororoti subiu a colina, houve um estrondo terrível, e os jovens aterrorizados viram de longe como seu professor desapareceu repentinamente no ar cercado por grandes línguas de pano, fumaça e forte trovão.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Setelah bertahun-tahun ia mendaki bukit Pukato-Ti; terdengar dentuman dahsyat, dan para pemuda melihat guru mereka lenyap ke udara di tengah asap dan guntur.",
        "en": "After years he climbed the hill of Pukato-Ti; there was a terrible roar, and the young men saw their teacher vanish into the air amid smoke and thunder."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bep-kororoti-c06",
      "source_id": "bep-kororoti-s1",
      "quote": "Nesta ocasião, um dos membros da tribo Kayapó está vestido da cabeça aos pés com uma roupa de palha cuidadosamente confeccionada segundo o bo de Bep-Kororoti.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam perayaan tahunan untuk mengenangnya, seorang anggota suku Kayapó dibalut dari kepala sampai kaki dengan pakaian jerami yang dibuat menurut bo (pakaian) Bep-Kororoti.",
        "en": "At the annual celebration in his memory, a Kayapó man is dressed head to foot in a straw costume made after Bep-Kororoti's bo (suit)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bep-kororoti-c07",
      "source_id": "bep-kororoti-s1",
      "quote": "Nota: A vestimenta também se assemelha a um traje de apicultor. Contrariando as teorias ufologas.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Artikel itu sendiri mencatat bahwa pakaian ritual ini juga mirip pakaian peternak lebah, berlawanan dengan teori ufologi.",
        "en": "The costume also resembles a beekeeper's suit, contrary to ufological theories."
      }
    }
  ],

    "id": "Berberoka adalah raksasa air rawa dari Luzon utara, Filipina, yang mengisap air kolam untuk memikat orang lalu menenggelamkan dan memangsa mereka.",
    "en": "The Berberoka is a swamp water ogre of northern Luzon, Philippines, that drains ponds to lure people, then drowns and eats them.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "berberoka-c01",
      "berberoka-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Berberoka adalah makhluk rawa yang konon muncul di Apayao, Abra, dan Ilocos Norte di Luzon utara, Filipina. Ia dapat mengisap seluruh air rawa atau kolam sehingga ikan tergeletak; saat orang datang memungut ikan, ia melepaskan airnya dan memangsa mereka yang tenggelam. Meski berkuasa, raksasa air ini takut pada kepiting.",
      "en": "The Berberoka is a swamp creature said to appear in Apayao, Abra and Ilocos Norte in northern Luzon, Philippines. It can suck up all the water of a swamp or pond, leaving the fish stranded; when people come to gather them, it releases the water and eats those who flounder. Despite its powers, this water ogre fears crabs.",

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Binatang berkepala tujuh adalah jenis monster, sering naga atau ular, yang muncul di banyak tradisi dunia, dari Kitab Wahyu sampai dongeng Kanada Prancis.",
    "en": "The seven-headed beast is a type of monster, often a dragon or serpent, found in many world traditions, from Revelation to French-Canadian tales.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "bete-a-sept-tetes-c01",
      "bete-a-sept-tetes-c02",
      "bete-a-sept-tetes-c06"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Binatang berkepala tujuh adalah jenis monster legenda, sering naga atau ular, yang ditemukan di banyak agama dan tradisi dunia; dalam beberapa tradisi kepalanya tumbuh kembali bila dipenggal. Ia muncul sebagai Binatang dalam Kitab Wahyu, yang tujuh kepalanya adalah tujuh bukit sekaligus tujuh raja, sebagai naga Hindu, sebagai Talafsa Berber yang meminta persembahan gadis untuk air, dan sebagai Herensugue Basque.",
      "en": "The seven-headed beast is a type of legendary monster, often a dragon or serpent, found in many world religions and traditions; in some, its heads grow back when cut off. It appears as the Beast of Revelation, whose seven heads are seven hills and seven kings, as the Hindu nāga, as the Berber Talafsa demanding a girl's sacrifice for water, and as the Basque Herensugue.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "bete-a-sept-tetes-c01",
        "bete-a-sept-tetes-c02",
        "bete-a-sept-tetes-c03",
        "bete-a-sept-tetes-c04"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Dalam dongeng rakyat ia gambaran klasik naga, misalnya dalam kisah Ti-Jean dari Kanada Prancis.",
      "en": "In folk tales it is a classic image of the dragon, as in the French-Canadian Ti-Jean stories.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "bete-a-sept-tetes-c05",
        "bete-a-sept-tetes-c06"
      ]
    }
  ],

  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "bete-a-sept-tetes-c01",
      "source_id": "bete-a-sept-tetes-s1",
      "quote": "La Bête à sept têtes est un type de monstre de légende qui se retrouve, sous des formes différentes (souvent un dragon ou un serpent à sept têtes) dans de nombreuses religions, mythologies et traditions à travers le monde. Dans plusieurs traditions, lorsqu'une tête est tranchée, elle repousse en un ou plusieurs exemplaires.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Bête à sept têtes (binatang berkepala tujuh) adalah jenis monster legenda yang muncul dalam berbagai bentuk, sering naga atau ular berkepala tujuh, di banyak agama, mitologi, dan tradisi dunia; dalam beberapa tradisi kepala yang dipenggal tumbuh kembali satu atau lebih.",
        "en": "The seven-headed beast is a type of legendary monster found in many forms, often a seven-headed dragon or serpent, in many religions, mythologies and traditions worldwide; in several traditions a severed head grows back, once or more."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bete-a-sept-tetes-c02",
      "source_id": "bete-a-sept-tetes-s1",
      "quote": "On retrouve la bête à sept têtes notamment dans la mythologie chrétienne (la Bête de l'Apocalypse), la mythologie hindoue (le nâga), la mythologie libyque (la Talafsa), la mythologie basque (Herensugue), ainsi que dans des contes populaires de divers pays",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia ditemukan antara lain dalam mitologi Kristen (Binatang dalam Kitab Wahyu), mitologi Hindu (naga), mitologi Libya (Talafsa), mitologi Basque (Herensugue), dan dongeng rakyat berbagai negeri.",
        "en": "It appears in Christian mythology (the Beast of the Apocalypse), Hindu mythology (the nāga), Libyan mythology (the Talafsa), Basque mythology (Herensugue) and folk tales of many countries."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bete-a-sept-tetes-c03",
      "source_id": "bete-a-sept-tetes-s1",
      "quote": "Selon l'Apocalypse (Ap 17. 9), ses sept têtes sont les sept collines [de Rome] mais aussi, simultanément, sept rois successifs",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut Kitab Wahyu (17:9), ketujuh kepalanya adalah tujuh bukit (Roma) sekaligus tujuh raja berturut-turut.",
        "en": "According to Revelation (17:9) its seven heads are the seven hills (of Rome) and at the same time seven successive kings."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bete-a-sept-tetes-c04",
      "source_id": "bete-a-sept-tetes-s1",
      "quote": "Dans la mythologie berbère, le dragon féminin à sept têtes, Talafsa, contrôle l'eau d'une région et ne la donne qu'en échange du sacrifice annuel d'une jeune fille.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam mitologi Berber, naga betina berkepala tujuh Talafsa menguasai air suatu daerah dan hanya memberikannya dengan imbalan persembahan seorang gadis setiap tahun.",
        "en": "In Berber mythology the female seven-headed dragon Talafsa controls a region's water and gives it only in exchange for the yearly sacrifice of a girl."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bete-a-sept-tetes-c05",
      "source_id": "bete-a-sept-tetes-s1",
      "quote": "La Bête à sept têtes est une figuration classique du dragon dans plusieurs contes populaires.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Binatang berkepala tujuh adalah gambaran klasik naga dalam banyak dongeng rakyat.",
        "en": "The seven-headed beast is a classic image of the dragon in many folk tales."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bete-a-sept-tetes-c06",
      "source_id": "bete-a-sept-tetes-s1",
      "quote": "Le conte Ti-Jean et le cheval blanc, conte de la Beauce ayant pour héros Ti-Jean, héros par excellence des contes du Canada français, évoque aussi la Bête à sept têtes.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dongeng Ti-Jean et le cheval blanc dari Beauce, dengan pahlawan dongeng Kanada Prancis Ti-Jean, juga menyebut binatang berkepala tujuh.",
        "en": "The Beauce tale Ti-Jean et le cheval blanc, featuring Ti-Jean, the archetypal hero of French-Canadian tales, also mentions the seven-headed beast."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Bicho Cidrão adalah arwah gembala dalam legenda Madeira yang menghantui gunung Montado do Cidrão dan teriakannya pertanda badai.",
    "en": "The Bicho Cidrão is a shepherd's ghost of a legend of Madeira haunting the Montado do Cidrão, whose cry foretells storms.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "bicho-cidrao-c01",
      "bicho-cidrao-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Bicho Cidrão adalah makhluk legenda Portugal di Madeira yang menghantui gunung Montado do Cidrão di Curral das Freiras. Menurut legenda ia arwah gentayangan seorang gembala. Teriakannya pertanda hujan atau badai, dan ia menggiring kawanan ternak tanpa terlihat.",
      "en": "The Bicho Cidrão is a being of Portuguese legend in Madeira that haunts the Montado do Cidrão in Curral das Freiras. Legend makes it the lost soul of a shepherd. Its cry foretells rain or storm, and it herds the flock unseen.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "bicho-cidrao-c01",
        "bicho-cidrao-c02"
      ]
    }
  ],

    {
      "id": "bicho-cidrao-c01",
      "source_id": "bicho-cidrao-s1",
      "quote": "O Bicho Cidrão é um ser imaginário das lendas portuguesas na região da Madeira que assombra uma montanha, na freguesia do Curral das Freiras, chamada o Montado do Cidrão.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Bicho Cidrão adalah makhluk khayalan legenda Portugal di Madeira yang menghantui gunung Montado do Cidrão di paroki Curral das Freiras.",
        "en": "The Bicho Cidrão is an imaginary being of Portuguese legend in Madeira that haunts a mountain called the Montado do Cidrão in the parish of Curral das Freiras."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bicho-cidrao-c02",
      "source_id": "bicho-cidrao-s1",
      "quote": "O bicho Cidrão seria, segundo a lenda, a alma penada de um pastor.  Conta-se que quando o bicho Cidrão berra é  presságio de chuva ou tempestade e que o bicho Cidrão  conduz o rebanho sem que ninguém veja.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut legenda ia arwah gentayangan seorang gembala; teriakannya pertanda hujan atau badai, dan ia menggiring kawanan ternak tanpa terlihat.",
        "en": "Legend says it is the lost soul of a shepherd; its cry foretells rain or storm, and it herds the flock unseen."
      }
    }
  ],
