```json
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Bida adalah ular raksasa pelindung kerajaan Wagadu dalam legenda Soninke yang menuntut persembahan gadis setiap tahun sampai dibunuh.",
    "en": "Bida is the giant serpent guarding the kingdom of Wagadu in Soninke legend, demanding a yearly maiden until he is slain.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "bida-c01",
      "bida-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Bida, \"piton\" dalam bahasa Soninke, adalah roh berwujud ular raksasa dalam legenda abad pertengahan kerajaan Wagadu, kembaran pahlawan pendiri Dinga. Ia melindungi Wagadu dan keluarga penguasa Cissé dengan imbalan persembahan seorang gadis berjubah putih yang menunggang kuda putih, biasanya setiap tahun pada akhir musim kemarau.",
      "en": "Bida, 'python' in Soninke, is a spirit in the form of a giant serpent in the medieval legend of the kingdom of Wagadu, twin of the founding hero Dinga. He protects Wagadu and the ruling Cissé family in return for the sacrifice of a girl in a white cloak on a white horse, usually yearly at the end of the dry season.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "bida-c01",
        "bida-c02",
        "bida-c05"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Akhirnya Amadou membunuhnya saat ia hendak memangsa korban. Sebelum mati Bida mengutuk negeri itu dengan tujuh tahun kekeringan, dan serpihan kepalanya menjadi tambang emas Bambouk dan Bouré. Kematiannya dipakai untuk menjelaskan kemunduran Wagadu.",
      "en": "Finally Amadou kills him as he is about to devour his victim. Dying, Bida curses the land with seven years of drought, and fragments of his head become the gold mines of Bambouk and Bouré. His death serves to explain Wagadu's decline.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "bida-c02",
        "bida-c03",
        "bida-c06",
        "bida-c07"
      ]
    }

  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "bida-c01",
      "source_id": "bida-s1",
      "quote": "Bida (« boa » ou « python » en soninké) est un esprit ayant la forme d'un serpent massif qui apparaît dans une légende médiévale liée à l'histoire du royaume du Ouagadou. Ce monstre reptile est décrit comme jumeau de Dinga, le héros fondateur du Ouagadou.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Bida (\"ular boa\" atau \"piton\" dalam bahasa Soninke) adalah roh berwujud ular raksasa dalam legenda abad pertengahan tentang kerajaan Wagadu, yang digambarkan sebagai kembaran Dinga, pahlawan pendiri Wagadu.",
        "en": "Bida ('boa' or 'python' in Soninke) is a spirit in the form of a massive serpent in a medieval legend tied to the history of the kingdom of Wagadu, described as the twin of Dinga, Wagadu's founding hero."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bida-c02",
      "source_id": "bida-s1",
      "quote": "Bida protège le Ouagadou et la famille régnante des Cissé en échange du sacrifice d'une jeune fille à intervalles réguliers, jusqu'au moment où il est tué par un homme appelé Amadou alors qu'il s'apprête à dévorer sa victime annuelle.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Bida melindungi Wagadu dan keluarga penguasa Cissé dengan imbalan persembahan seorang gadis secara berkala, sampai ia dibunuh seorang lelaki bernama Amadou saat hendak memangsa korban tahunannya.",
        "en": "Bida protects Wagadu and the ruling Cissé family in exchange for the regular sacrifice of a girl, until he is killed by a man called Amadou as he is about to devour his yearly victim."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bida-c03",
      "source_id": "bida-s1",
      "quote": "La mort du serpent tutélaire est généralement employée par le récit pour expliquer le déclin du royaume.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kematian ular pelindung ini biasanya dipakai cerita untuk menjelaskan kemunduran kerajaan.",
        "en": "The death of the guardian serpent is generally used by the story to explain the kingdom's decline."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bida-c04",
      "source_id": "bida-s1",
      "quote": "Le serpent possède de nombreux noms différents selon les versions et les langues : Bida ou Miniyan en soninké, Sâba en mandinka, ou encore Wagadou Sa ba, « grand serpent du Ouagadou ».",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ular itu punya banyak nama: Bida atau Miniyan dalam bahasa Soninke, Sâba dalam bahasa Mandinka, atau Wagadou Sa ba, \"ular agung Wagadu\".",
        "en": "The serpent has many names: Bida or Miniyan in Soninke, Sâba in Mandinka, or Wagadou Sa ba, \"great serpent of Wagadu\"."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bida-c05",
      "source_id": "bida-s1",
      "quote": "Parfois le sacrifice a lieu chaque année à la fin de la saison sèche, et la jeune fille doit être vêtue d'un manteau blanc et montée sur un cheval blanc.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kadang persembahan dilakukan setiap tahun pada akhir musim kemarau, dan gadis itu harus berjubah putih dan menunggang kuda putih.",
        "en": "Sometimes the sacrifice takes place every year at the end of the dry season, with the girl dressed in a white cloak and riding a white horse."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bida-c06",
      "source_id": "bida-s1",
      "quote": "Avant de mourir, le serpent profère une terrible malédiction contre le pays, en promettant sept années de sécheresse.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sebelum mati, ular itu mengutuk negeri itu dengan tujuh tahun kekeringan.",
        "en": "Before dying, the serpent lays a terrible curse on the land, promising seven years of drought."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bida-c07",
      "source_id": "bida-s1",
      "quote": "Les débris de la tête de Bida retombent dans le Bambouk et le Bouré et s'y changent en mines d'or qui font prospérer l'empire du Ghana par la suite.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Serpihan kepala Bida jatuh di Bambouk dan Bouré dan berubah menjadi tambang emas yang kemudian memakmurkan Kekaisaran Ghana.",
        "en": "Fragments of Bida's head fall in Bambouk and Bouré and turn into gold mines that later make the Ghana Empire prosper."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],
  "gaps": [
    {
      "field": "sources",
      "searched": "Hanya tersedia artikel Wikipedia fr untuk makhluk ini; belum ditemukan sumber kedua yang dapat dikutip."
    },
    {
      "field": "images",
      "searched": "Gambar Commons belum dicari pada putaran ini; dijadwalkan pada pemeriksaan gambar terpisah."
    }
  ]
}
```

```json
  "traits": [],
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Biddrina adalah ular raksasa bersisik bercahaya dalam legenda Caltanissetta, Sisilia, yang menelan ternak dan anak-anak di daerah rawa.",
    "en": "The biddrina is a giant shining-scaled serpent of the legends of Caltanissetta, Sicily, that swallows livestock and children in marshes.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "biddrina-c01",
      "biddrina-c02",
      "biddrina-c03"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Biddrina adalah hewan mitos yang hidup di daerah basah pedesaan Caltanissetta, di beberapa tempat disebut Culobbia. Reptil buas ini berwarna hijau-biru, bermata merah, bermulut cukup besar untuk menelan anak domba dan anak-anak, dan berlapis sisik bercahaya yang hampir tak dapat dihancurkan. Konon ular yang bersembunyi tujuh tahun berubah menjadi biddrina.",
      "en": "The biddrina is a mythical animal of the wetlands of the Caltanissetta countryside, called Culobbia in some places. This fierce reptile is green-blue, red-eyed, with a mouth big enough to swallow lambs and children, and armoured in near-indestructible shining scales. A snake that hides for seven years is said to become a biddrina.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "biddrina-c01",
        "biddrina-c02",
        "biddrina-c03",
        "biddrina-c04",
        "biddrina-c06"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Di Butera orang mengarak boneka naga u sirpintazzu pada malam pesta San Rocco untuk memperingati terbunuhnya biddrina. Makhluk ini mungkin diciptakan untuk menjauhkan anak-anak dari kolam rawa.",
      "en": "In Butera people parade the dragon puppet u sirpintazzu on the eve of San Rocco to commemorate a biddrina's killing. The creature was probably invented to keep children away from marshy ponds.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "biddrina-c05",
        "biddrina-c07"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "cultural_context": null,

    }
  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "biddrina-c01",
      "source_id": "biddrina-s1",
      "quote": "La biddrina è un animale mitico che secondo la leggenda vive nelle zone umide della campagna della provincia di Caltanissetta.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Biddrina adalah hewan mitos yang menurut legenda hidup di daerah basah pedesaan provinsi Caltanissetta.",
        "en": "The biddrina is a mythical animal that legend places in the wetlands of the countryside of the province of Caltanissetta."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "biddrina-c02",
      "source_id": "biddrina-s1",
      "quote": "Il termine Biddrina potrebbe derivare da una parola araba che indicherebbe un grosso serpente d'acqua. Secondo altre fonti, invece, potrebbe originare da \"belluino\", bestiale. In alcune zone della Sicilia viene chiamata Culobbia o \"Culofia\".",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Nama Biddrina mungkin berasal dari kata Arab untuk ular air besar, atau dari \"belluino\", buas; di beberapa daerah Sisilia ia disebut Culobbia atau Culofia.",
        "en": "The name Biddrina may come from an Arabic word for a big water snake, or from 'belluino', bestial; in some parts of Sicily it is called Culobbia or Culofia."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "biddrina-c03",
      "source_id": "biddrina-s1",
      "quote": "Questo ferocissimo rettile avrebbe una colorazione tra il verde e il blu, occhi rossi e una bocca talmente grande da consentirgli di ingoiare capretti, agnelli e bambini.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Reptil yang sangat buas ini berwarna antara hijau dan biru, bermata merah, dan bermulut begitu besar sehingga dapat menelan anak kambing, anak domba, dan anak-anak.",
        "en": "This ferocious reptile is green-blue with red eyes and a mouth big enough to swallow kids, lambs and children."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "biddrina-c04",
      "source_id": "biddrina-s1",
      "quote": "Possiede una robusta corazza di squame luminose che la rende praticamente indistruttibile.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia berlapis sisik bercahaya yang kuat sehingga hampir tak dapat dihancurkan.",
        "en": "It has a sturdy armour of shining scales that makes it practically indestructible."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "biddrina-c05",
      "source_id": "biddrina-s1",
      "quote": "Attualmente a Butera, la vigilia della festa di San Rocco, è costume portare in giro per le strade u sirpintazzu, uno spauracchio in carta pesta simile per fattezze al drago della tradizione cinese, proprio per ricordare l'uccisione di una Biddrina",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di Butera, pada malam pesta San Rocco, orang mengarak u sirpintazzu, boneka kertas mirip naga Tiongkok, untuk memperingati terbunuhnya sebuah Biddrina.",
        "en": "In Butera, on the eve of the feast of San Rocco, people parade u sirpintazzu, a papier-mâché bogey like a Chinese dragon, to commemorate the killing of a Biddrina."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "biddrina-c06",
      "source_id": "biddrina-s1",
      "quote": "La leggenda vuole che una biscia che rimanga nascosta per sette anni si tramuta in biddrina, diventando gigantesca come per magia.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut legenda, ular yang bersembunyi selama tujuh tahun berubah menjadi biddrina dan tumbuh raksasa seperti disihir.",
        "en": "Legend says a grass snake that stays hidden for seven years turns into a biddrina, growing gigantic as if by magic."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "biddrina-c07",
      "source_id": "biddrina-s1",
      "quote": "L'invenzione di questa creatura rispondeva probabilmente all'esigenza di evitare che i bambini andassero a fare il bagno in questi laghetti paludosi col pericolo di annegarvi.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Makhluk ini mungkin diciptakan agar anak-anak tidak mandi di kolam rawa dan tenggelam.",
        "en": "The creature was probably invented to keep children from bathing in these marshy ponds and drowning."
      }
    }
  ],
```

```json
      "id": "Biliku adalah makhluk legendaris yang dipuja di Kepulauan Andaman, sering digambarkan sebagai perempuan laba-laba raksasa. Ia lalu pindah ke bumi membawa api dan cahaya, dan mengatur segala hal, terutama cuaca, menurut suasana hatinya.",

      "source_id": "biliku-s1",
      "quote": "Biliku è una creatura leggendaria, venerata nelle isole Andamane, spesso raffigurata come una donna-ragno.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Biliku adalah makhluk legendaris yang dipuja di Kepulauan Andaman, sering digambarkan sebagai perempuan laba-laba.",
        "en": "Biliku is a legendary being worshipped in the Andaman Islands, often depicted as a spider-woman."

    {
      "id": "biliku-c02",
      "source_id": "biliku-s1",
      "quote": "Si narra che esistesse prima della creazione del mondo e che un giorno creò la Terra e gli uomini: dopo aver tessuto la sua tela, si aggirò furtiva e a grandi passi nella \"vasta notte vuota\"",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Konon ia ada sebelum dunia diciptakan dan suatu hari menciptakan bumi dan manusia: setelah menenun jaringnya, ia melangkah diam-diam dalam \"malam kosong yang luas\".",
        "en": "She is said to have existed before the world's creation and one day created the Earth and humans: after weaving her web, she stalked through the 'vast empty night'."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "biliku-c03",
      "source_id": "biliku-s1",
      "quote": "Contenta della sua creazione vi si trasferì, portandovi il fuoco e la luce e governandone ogni aspetto (in particolare il tempo atmosferico) a seconda del suo umore",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Puas dengan ciptaannya, ia pindah ke bumi membawa api dan cahaya, dan mengatur segala hal, terutama cuaca, menurut suasana hatinya.",
        "en": "Pleased with her creation, she moved there bringing fire and light, governing everything, especially the weather, according to her mood."
      }
    }
```

```json
      "source_id": "bisso-galeto-s1",
      "quote": "Il Bisso Galeto Meglio chiamato El Cicolin Giacomo è una creatura leggendaria delle valli veronesi dalla Lessinia fino a Perzacco. Ha corpo e testa di gallo (galeto), con una grande cresta rossa, ali piene di spine e coda di serpente (bisso).",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {

        "en": "The Bisso Galeto, also called El Cicolin Giacomo, is a legendary creature of the Verona valleys from Lessinia to Perzacco, with the body and head of a cockerel (galeto), a big red comb, spiny wings and a snake's tail (bisso)."

      "id": "bisso-galeto-c02",
      "source_id": "bisso-galeto-s1",
      "quote": "il Bisso Galeto può aumentare e diminuire la lunghezza del proprio corpo a sua volontà. È un animale velenosissimo. Nascerebbe da un uovo deposto da un gallo vecchio e covato da un serpente o da un rospo per nove anni.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia dapat memanjang dan memendekkan tubuhnya sesuka hati, sangat beracun, dan konon menetas dari telur ayam jantan tua yang dierami ular atau kodok selama sembilan tahun.",
        "en": "It can lengthen or shorten its body at will, is extremely venomous, and is said to hatch from an old cockerel's egg brooded by a snake or toad for nine years."

      "source_id": "bisso-galeto-s1",
      "quote": "Lo sguardo del Bisso Galeto può uccidere, seccare le piante e contaminare l'acqua. Il fiato e il morso del Bisso Galeto sono mortali.",
      "locator": null,

      "statement": {

      "quote": "Se il Bisso vede la propria immagine in uno specchio muore.",

      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {

        "en": "If the Bisso sees its own reflection in a mirror, it dies."

    {
      "id": "bisso-galeto-c05",
      "source_id": "bisso-galeto-s1",
      "quote": "Gli unici \"nemici naturali\" del Bisso Galeto sono le donnole, che però possono ucciderlo solo a costo della propria vita e i galli che riescono a far morire il Bisso con il proprio canto.",
      "locator": null,
```
